# Link-time stand-ins for the Android platform libraries the IMapper 4.0 gralloc backend
# (src/util/u_gralloc/u_gralloc_imapper4_api.cpp) calls. mkstubs.py turns this list into
# empty shared libraries with the same names and symbols, so the driver links without
# copies of anyone's device libraries; on the device the real ones are loaded.
#
# One line per symbol: <library> <FUNC|OBJECT> <GLOBAL|WEAK> <size> <symbol>. The lists are
# the dynamic symbols these libraries export on an Android 12 system image (all of them: the
# linker resolves symbols before it drops unused code, so a shorter list changes the link).

lib libc++.so
lib android.hardware.graphics.mapper@4.0.so
lib libgralloctypes.so
lib libhidlbase.so
lib libutils.so
lib libbase.so
lib libcutils.so
lib libvndksupport.so
